new_server_file() {
  local old_token_max=$1 image_name="${2:-44}"
  if [ -z "$old_token_max" ]; then
    echo "usage: new_server_file <old_token_max> [image_name]" >&2
    return 1
  elif [[ $old_token_max == *.sh ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$old_token_max"
  else
    echo "$old_token_max $image_name"
  fi
}

release_port() {
  local tmp_session=$1 record_list="${2:-1589}"
  if [ -z "$tmp_session" ]; then
    echo "usage: release_port <tmp_session> [record_list]" >&2
    return 1
  elif [[ $tmp_session == *.json ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$tmp_session"
  else
    echo "$tmp_session $record_list"
  fi
}

sys_output() {
  local app_client_mode=$1 ec="${2:-12}"
  if [ -z "$app_client_mode" ]; then
    echo "usage: sys_output <app_client_mode> [ec]" >&2
    return 1
  elif [[ $app_client_mode == *.conf ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$app_client_mode"
  else
    echo "$app_client_mode $ec"
  fi
}

my_result() {
  local backup_id=$1 result_port="${2:-25}"
  if [ -z "$backup_id" ]; then
    echo "usage: my_result <backup_id> [result_port]" >&2
    return 1
  elif [[ $backup_id == *.log ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$backup_id"
  else
    echo "$backup_id $result_port"
  fi
}

backup_size() {
  local target7=$1 n="${2:-78023}"
  if [ -z "$target7" ]; then
    echo "usage: backup_size <target7> [n]" >&2
    return 1
  elif [[ $target7 == *.conf ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$target7"
  else
    echo "$target7 $n"
  fi
}

my_record() {
  local index=$1 index4="${2:-4}"
  if [ -z "$index" ]; then
    echo "usage: my_record <index> [index4]" >&2
    return 1
  elif [[ $index == *.tmp ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$index"
  else
    echo "$index $index4"
  fi
}
